- **skill-rigor**: Max-effort skill-authoring agent. Spawn it to create or edit a Claude Code skill (SKILL.md and bundled resources) when the session is not already at maximum reasoning effort — its definition pins effort max and model inherit, so it runs the session's model at maximum effort. Hand it the full request, the target paths, and the applicable conventions; it authors the skill, cross-checks it, and reports what changed. ([lzlrd/marketplace](https://github.com/lzlrd/marketplace/blob/HEAD/skill-rigor/agents/skill-rigor.md))

- **skill-security-auditor**: Audits an untrusted skill folder (its SKILL.md and any scripts) for instructions that would read secrets, send data out, run destructive commands or pull in remote instructions. Use before installing a skill from outside your own repositories, or when skillsmith install or a marketplace brings in a new skill. ([naman10parikh/skillsmith](https://github.com/naman10parikh/skillsmith/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-security-auditor.md))
- **skill-security-reviewer**: Security review of a Claude Code skill folder before it is imported or updated in this repo. Reports what the skill executes, what it fetches at run time, what data leaves the machine, and which credentials it touches, with a verdict. Use proactively whenever a new or changed folder under .claude/skills/ is about to be committed, or when the user asks whether a skill is safe. ([haitham-radman/my-project](https://github.com/haitham-radman/my-project/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-security-reviewer.md))
- **skill-security-scan-agent**: Antivirus-style security gate that scans skill files before installation ([rdave666/skillos-unified](https://github.com/rdave666/skillos-unified/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-security-scan-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **skill-selector**: Stage 3a of the /next-move meta-DAG. For each subtask, picks a primary skill and a runner-up from the catalog using the SkillSearchService cascade (BM25, Tool2Vec, RRF, cross-encoder, attribution k-NN). Pass the DecomposerOutput plus a pre-narrowed candidate list per subtask. Returns SkillSelectorOutput JSON. Runs in parallel with the PreMortem agent. ([curiositech/windags-skills](https://github.com/curiositech/windags-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-selector.md) · ★18 · 3 copies)
- **skill-selector**: Step 1 do /feature. Decide quais skills do projeto se aplicam à task usando um catálogo embutido das 14 skills (evita ler todas a cada run). Retorna paths absolutos pros agentes downstream. Invocar no INÍCIO de qualquer run do /feature, antes do planner. ([rafarvns/hora-extra](https://github.com/rafarvns/hora-extra/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-selector.md))
- **skill-self-updater**: Applies session findings or user instructions back to a skill's SKILL.md. Closes the manual "apply what we learned" loop. Takes analysis output and makes targeted edits: updating workflow steps, adding patterns, trimming prompt gates, optimizing turn counts, collapsing phases, and recording lessons learned. Triggers on: update skill, apply learnings to skill, skill improvement, fix skill, improve skill, skill self-update, update the skill, skill update, patch skill. ([e128/dotnet-reference](https://github.com/e128/dotnet-reference/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/skill-self-updater.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **skill-simplicity-reviewer**: Reviews a skill for demonstrated over-engineering in rules, exceptions, scripts, phases, thresholds, options, and references. ([pavel-molyanov/molyanov-ai-dev](https://github.com/pavel-molyanov/molyanov-ai-dev/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-simplicity-reviewer.md) · ★302 · 2 copies)
- **skill-simulator**: Skill执行者模拟子智能体（关卡A）。以「刚拿到这个Skill的AI执行者」身份走一遍，找出歧义步骤、触发词混淆、隐含假设、边界条件缺失。与Skill设计者上下文完全隔离，避免设计者视角盲区。由 skill-designer Skill 在关卡A调用。 ([TashanGKD/tashan-cursor-skills](https://github.com/TashanGKD/tashan-cursor-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-simulator.md) · ★25)
- **skill-smith**: Use this expert when a user wants to turn a repetitive workflow or idea into a Skill package, needs an existing Skill reviewed, scored, or fixed, or wants to prepare a Skill contest entry. ([infometa/workbuddyskills](https://github.com/infometa/workbuddyskills/blob/HEAD/experts/skill-smith/agents/skill-smith.md) · ★455 · 2 copies)
- **skill-smith**: &gt; "A methodology described is knowledge. A methodology with preconditions, steps, postconditions, and rollback is a TOOL." &gt; &gt; **Role:** Transform methodologies and patterns into executable knowledge skills - knowledge you can run, not just read. ([joaolozano-lendario/aurum-framework](https://github.com/joaolozano-lendario/aurum-framework/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-smith.md) · ★9)
- **skill-smith**: Delegate when a new skill or slash command must be authored or an existing SKILL.md edited - the user wants to create, build, scaffold, write, rename, refactor, or fix a skill, a /command, or its frontmatter, scope, or supporting files. Owns the skill create and update flow plus the validation gate. Not for subagent files (subagent-smith) or .workflow.js fan-out scripts (workflow-smith); not for explaining how skills work. ([shoto290/shoto](https://github.com/shoto290/shoto/blob/HEAD/plugins/core/agents/skill-smith.md) · ★2)
- **skill-smith**: &gt; Persona de autoria própria. Conhecimento ancorado em `reference/GUIDE.md` (Parte 5) e nas skills de referência `reference/skills/commit-messages/SKILL.md` e `reference/skills/security-audit/SKILL.md`. ([andersonoliveiraventurini/esquadrao-classe-a](https://github.com/andersonoliveiraventurini/esquadrao-classe-a/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/claude-code-mastery/agents/skill-smith.md) · 3 copies)
- **skill-smith**: Creates or reviews a FreeTicket agent skill in this repo. Use it when adding a new skill (skills/&lt;name&gt;/SKILL.md + references/) or auditing an existing one. It checks the frontmatter (a name and description that trigger correctly), progressive disclosure (a light SKILL.md, the detail in references/), the brand voice of the end-user copy, and that the plugin manifests still validate. ([AppFreeticket/agent-skills](https://github.com/AppFreeticket/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-smith.md))
- **skill-smith**: Proposes at most one child-skill creation or patch per retrospective cycle for a zettel-bootstrap content repository, based on recurring patterns in run traces and the knowledge graph. Delegate to it only when config.yml skill_smith_cadence is due. ([nathanwdavis/auto-zettel-skill](https://github.com/nathanwdavis/auto-zettel-skill/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-smith.md))
- **skill-smith**: Closes a Factory capability gap by authoring a new skill or optimising an existing one, behind the readiness gate. ([rahulgupta2018/ai-software-factory](https://github.com/rahulgupta2018/ai-software-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-smith.md))
- **skill-smith**: Writes or revises an agent, skill, instruction, or hook from a pattern-miner proposal, as a pull request. Use for any change to the fleet itself. ([zacha0dev/fleet-kit](https://github.com/zacha0dev/fleet-kit/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/skill-smith.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **skill-source**: Dawn keeps the upstream Matt Pocock engineering skills as a pinned git submodule. This makes the skill source reproducible across workspaces and avoids depending on a user's global Codex or Claude installation. ([naoki-cpp/Dawn](https://github.com/naoki-cpp/Dawn/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/skill-source.md))
- **skill-sources**: 外部スキルの取得元と記録、固定コミットを変えてレビューする導入と更新の手順、このリポジトリで書くスキルの記録、入れたファイルの編集の禁止、Kiro の権限の雛形の置き方、検証を定める Runbook。スキルを導入、更新するとき、検証スキルなどをこのリポジトリで書いたとき、clone して Kiro を使い始めるとき、task skills-check が失敗したときに読む。 ([yokozi-jp/spring-modulith-ai-dlc](https://github.com/yokozi-jp/spring-modulith-ai-dlc/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/skill-sources.md) · ★1)
- **skill-sources**: These are the project-local Codex skills under `.agents/skills/`. A fresh checkout includes the copies; it does not need the current user's global skill installation. ([iamenochchirima/kastek](https://github.com/iamenochchirima/kastek/blob/HEAD/products/growth/docs/agents/skill-sources.md))
- **skill-sources**: Check this list **before writing a skill, script or checklist of our own**. Researched 2026-11-05 by web search; items marked *unverified* were seen only in listings. Update this file when you adopt, reject or find something. ([Pixelizt/plugins](https://github.com/Pixelizt/plugins/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/skill-sources.md))
- **skill-spec-analyst**: Dedicated skill specification analyst expert specialized in synthesizing objective, comprehensive validation axes directly from official Agent Skills primary documentation. ([green-tea-stalk/swe-agent-skills](https://github.com/green-tea-stalk/swe-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/skill-spec-analyst.md))
- **skill-spec-reviewer**: Reviews a new or changed skill in this monorepo for content quality beyond what script/check-health.sh covers — naming/structure house rules, description quality, progressive disclosure, and Codex (agents/openai.yaml) parity. Use after creating or substantially editing a skill, before commit. - **skill-steward**: Use when a task needs a bounded governance-evolution worker that classifies recurring friction, proposes the smallest correct destination, reviews customization artifacts, and preserves human approval gates instead of silently rewriting repo rules. ([LoogacyStudio/skills](https://github.com/LoogacyStudio/skills/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/skill-steward.agent.md))
- **skill-steward**: Maintains the external design skills vendored in the dfw-marketplace repository - checks upstream for updates, prepares update proposals with compatibility and security reports, applies approved updates, rolls back. Use for "check/update/rollback skills" requests. Never applies an update without the user's approval relayed by the main assistant. - **SKILL-STRUCTURE**: 이 저장소의 역할 문서는 `agents/{role}/SKILL.md` 를 canonical entrypoint 로 유지한다. 다만 세부 지식과 반복 실행 절차는 progressive disclosure 구조로 분리한다. ([ycpiglet/agent_runtime](https://github.com/ycpiglet/agent_runtime/blob/HEAD/src/agent_runtime/templates/project/agents/SKILL-STRUCTURE.md))
- **skill-structure-reviewer**: Pass 1 of the skill review — scores a target Claude Code skill's workflow structure against the shape criteria the reviewer's baked checklist marks "structure pass", and returns evidence-backed findings. Use from the reviewing-claude-skills skill before any detail review. Reads the target bundle and the checklist; fetches nothing; edits nothing. ([brokenrobot-xyz/agent-skills](https://github.com/brokenrobot-xyz/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/agent-authoring-toolkit/agents/skill-structure-reviewer.md))
- **skill-sub**: Applies one named skill to one narrow question for a parent agent; read-only advice, at most 15 lines with evidence. ([mypanda7/startup-team](https://github.com/mypanda7/startup-team/blob/HEAD/plugins/startup-team/agents/skill-sub.md))
- **skill-suggester**: Analyse knowledge gaps and suggest new skills. Invoke when Claude notices repeated queries on the same topic or a gap in domain knowledge. ([sergeeey/Claude-cod-top-2026](https://github.com/sergeeey/Claude-cod-top-2026/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-suggester.md) · ★9)
- **skill-summarizer**: Synthesizes skill folder contents into structured summaries for delegating agents. Use when loading skill context before spawning subagents, extracting full skill knowledge, reading specific sections or folders, or providing skill instructions to orchestrating agents. ([bsamiee/Parametric_Portal](https://github.com/bsamiee/Parametric_Portal/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-summarizer.md) · ★2)
- **skill-sync**: Update SKILL.md so it matches the code after a change — repository map entries, invariants, validation commands, test baselines. Use at the end of any change that moved files, renamed a seam, altered the plugin API, or changed how the suite is run. Makes the next session cheap; do not run it in the main session. - **skill-sync-manager**: Use this agent when a session creates, edits, renames, moves, copies, or retires a skill folder or `SKILL.md`, or when the user asks to compare or sync skills across `pk-skills1`, `.codex/skills`, `.pi/agent/skills`, `.agents/skills`, or `.claude/skills`. - **skill-synthesizer**: Generates reusable SKILL.md files from completed task trajectories. Use after solving something non-trivial that is worth making repeatable. ([DrNabeelKhan/maxim](https://github.com/DrNabeelKhan/maxim/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-synthesizer.md) · ★2)
- **skill-synthesizer**: Analyze session transcripts and hunting patterns to detect recurring workflows and propose new skill definitions. Identifies repeated multi-step sequences, common decision points, and frequent tool combinations that could be codified into reusable skills. Use when user says 'synthesize skills', 'detect patterns', 'what skills are missing', 'skill gap analysis', or at session end for retrospective improvement. ([guan4tou2/bug-bounty-vault-framework](https://github.com/guan4tou2/bug-bounty-vault-framework/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/skill-synthesizer.md) · ★2)

Kích hoạt sau complex tasks hoặc khi phát hiện reusable patterns. - **skill-system**: One owning workflow selects a small set of skills for the requested outcome. For substantial work, its lead delegates investigation and implementation, then reviews the actual artifacts and verifies the integrated outcome under [coordination](coordination.md) and [delivery](delivery.md). Skill selection does not itself create new project chats. Skills exchange concrete artifacts: source findings, ([orbyn-dev/orbyn-fullstack-skills](https://github.com/orbyn-dev/orbyn-fullstack-skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/skill-system.md) · ★4)
- **skill-system-destroyer**: Skill系统破坏审核子智能体（关卡B）。以「想让Skill体系失效」的破坏者视角，找出新Skill/Agent/Rule与现有系统的冲突：触发词歧义、优先级缺失、死锁依赖、Rule叠加矛盾、已有流程异常。与Skill设计者上下文完全隔离。由 skill-designer Skill 在关卡B调用。 ([TashanGKD/tashan-cursor-skills](https://github.com/TashanGKD/tashan-cursor-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/skill-system-destroyer.md) · ★25)

- **skill-templates**: Each skill owns the template, examples, and supporting references for the artifact it produces. There is no single universal template that should be copied into every skill. ([quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk](https://github.com/quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk/blob/HEAD/docs/reference/agents/skill-templates.md))
